HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001305/B001","root_001332/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_01fe6c3816bc733f6f65","text":"5. **Place/rank is also denied to any rival.** Lexical evidence: `ك و ن B002` (place, position, rank), `س م و B001/B007` (rising/noble status; rivaling upward), `ك ف ء B001`, and `ء ح د B002`. Structural cue: the opening `بسم` raises a designation; 112:2 permits a chief-like resort image; 112:4 negates any equal \"for Him.\" Assigned roles: `ك و ن B002` supplies the space/rank slot where a rival might stand; `س م و` supplies upward competition; `كفوًا أحد` denies a matching occupant. Abductive move: I infer that `لم يكن له` is not only existence but no available station/rank opposite Him. Reading change: the ayah reads as removal of every rival place in the hierarchy: no one has a standing that can face Him as equal.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001332/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_0779d7fa37a1aa7260f7","text":"2. **No one occupies a matching position or rank.** The fixed occurrence root also offers `ك و ن:B002` (place, position, or rank), while fixed `ك ف ء:B001` supplies matching equality. In 112:2, `ص م د:B001` includes the chief or relied-on one to whom affairs and needs are directed, establishing a functional station before the final comparison. Position supplies the comparison field and counterpart supplies the failed candidate. The abductive move is that the later equality test concerns functional rank as well as bare likeness; the packet juxtaposes these branches but does not explicitly name rank as the object compared. The clause can thus deny any occupant of a station matching the relied-on one.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000017/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_1c0ebf204dc382491371","text":"3. **The final `أحد` reverses the first: from singular title to exhausted search.** Lexical evidence: 112:1 `ء ح د B001` (absolute one) and 112:4 `ء ح د B002` (negative exhaustive anyone). Structural cue: the surah forms an inclusio around `أحد`: first after `الله`, last after `كفوًا`. Assigned roles: the first `أحد` gives the positive identity; the last `أحد` runs through the possible field of addressable others and finds none. Abductive move: I infer a search procedure: the closing syntax operationalizes the opening doctrine by trying and exhausting the class of possible peers. Reading change: `أحد` becomes dynamic: it first asserts uniqueness, then performs the absence of any matching other.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001305/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_562ed25e2f6a30df7d29","text":"4. **Relation remains, but parity does not.** Fixed `ك ف ء:B001` negates equality, mutual equivalence, and matching response; it does not by itself negate every relation. The earlier packet supplies two asymmetric relations: fixed 112:0 `ر ح م:B001` is beneficence toward one shown mercy, and 112:2 `ص م د:B001` directs the needy toward the relied-on one. Those branches provide care and resort, while `كُفُوًا` removes peer reciprocity. I infer that the relations are coherent precisely because their roles differ, although the packet does not state this causal explanation. The ayah consequently reads as a denial of symmetrical counterpart status, not as a declaration of isolation from recipients, worshippers, or dependents.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001305/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_5f00daf625f9fdf329e3","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"112:4:4\",\"root_001305\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Soy bağlarının reddinden sonra denklik, eş olmaya uygun bir karşı taraf ihtimalini de kapatır; böylece aile düzeninin yatay eşlik modeli de Allah'a uygulanmaz.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000017/B001","root_000017/B002","root_001305/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_65ef5a1b68286609ca77","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"112:4:4\",\"root_001305\",[\"B001\"]],[\"112:4:5\",\"root_000017\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Sondaki hiç kimse ifadesi, ilk ayetteki birliği bütün muhtemel adaylar arasında sınar ve denk olabilecek tek bir aday bile bırakmaz.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001305/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_6b47e8e0e37afc23ce00","text":"4. **Tilt, reversal, and irregular rhyme remain excluded from the final balance.** Lexical evidence: `ك ف ء B002` (tilting, overturning, diverting), `ك ف ء B003` (irregular rhyme variation), and prior `ق و ل B001/B016` (spoken definition). Structural cue: the final clause arrives after four very short, balanced assertions/negations and after the command to say. Assigned roles: `ك ف ء B002/B003` provide latent images of imbalance, deviation, and mismatched correspondence; the surah's compressed repeated negatives enforce straightness and non-variation. Abductive move: I infer a formal analogy from lexical \"irregular correspondence/tilt\" to the poem-like sonic and syntactic closure; the packet supplies the branches and compact sequence, while I supply the aesthetic-mechanistic link. Reading change: the final denial can be heard as a refusal of any skewed counterpart or mismatched answering line: the utterance closes without an equivalent rhyme in reality.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001332/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_7814ec5902bb88ac6f43","text":"1. **No counterpart ever occupies the field of being.** Lexical evidence: `ك و ن B001` (being/occurrence/temporal predication), `ك ف ء B001` (equal counterpart), and `ء ح د B002` (negative exhaustive anyone). Structural cue: the clause combines `لم يكن` with an indefinite `كفوًا` and an indefinite `أحد` under negation, after the earlier affirmative `أحد`. Assigned roles: `يكن` opens the temporal/existential field; `كفوًا` names the matching relation sought within that field; `أحد` exhausts possible persons. Abductive move: I infer that negated occurrence plus exhaustive anyone creates more than a present comparison: it denies that such a counterpart has ever entered the relevant field. Reading change: 112:4 reads as an existential-temporal sweep: no equal counterpart has been, occurred, or stood for Him.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000017/B001","root_000017/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_841a74626ef4147da602","text":"5. **The opening One becomes an exhaustive closing test.** Fixed final `أَحَدٌ` is licensed by `ء ح د:B002` as exhaustive anyone in a negative context. Its structural counterpart is 112:1's positive `أَحَدٌ`, anchored there in `ء ح د:B001` as absolute oneness; between them stand reliance, two birth negations, and the equality term. The closing occurrence quantifies over every possible candidate, while the opening occurrence predicates the subject's unity. The inferred ring relation makes the last word operationalize the first: absolute oneness is tested by searching the whole candidate class for a peer. The clause therefore closes not with a second numerical \"one,\" but with \"anyone whatsoever\" failing the test of equivalence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001305/B002","root_001305/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_84a7cab293b337f8f6f5","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"112:4:4\",\"root_001305\",[\"B002\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Denkliğin reddinin, sözün biçiminde de eğrilme veya düzensiz bir karşılık bırakmayan dengeli bir kapanışla yankılandığı düşünülebilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001332/B001","root_001683/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_90bd6901da51163d1b36","text":"1. **No counterpart ever enters the field of occurrence.** Fixed-ayah `يَكُنْ` directly activates `ك و ن:B001` (being, occurrence, or temporal predication), and `كُفُوًا` directly activates `ك ف ء:B001` (equal counterpart). The preceding ayah supplies birth events through `و ل د:B003`, while this ayah shifts from denying those events to denying that an equal has been present or occurred at all. `ك و ن:B001` gives the equality claim a temporal field, and `لَمْ` closes that field negatively. I infer that the clause ranges beyond a present comparison because its occurrence verb follows two similarly negated event clauses. It therefore reads not merely \"there is no current equal,\" but \"no equal has ever entered the domain of being predicated for Him.\"","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001305/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_9875108ce880ca243c1a","text":"3. **The genealogy sequence closes by removing a mate-like counterpart.** Fixed `ك ف ء:B001` explicitly includes suitability in marriage as one scope of equal counterpart. Its immediate predecessor, 112:3, negates both sides of `و ل د:B001-B003`, making parent, offspring, and birth relations salient. The birth ayah removes vertical lineage; the marriage scope of `كُفُوًا` removes a horizontal peer from whom such a lineage mechanism could be socially modeled. I infer this family-system relation from adjacency and the accepted marriage scope, not from an explicit mention of a spouse. The final ayah therefore extends the denial of offspring and origin into denial of any equal mate-like counterpart.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_bfce1c2fb066b5d45baf","text":"The earlier `أحد` in 112:1 was the key retrospective activator: it made the final `أحد` read as the practical exhaustion of a field first named as absolute oneness. 112:2 also changed `كفوًا`: a counterpart would not merely resemble God, but would interrupt the one-way resort structure of `الصمد`. Finally, the basmalah's `س م و B005` and `ر ح م` branches sharpened the closing denial: no namesake/equal shares the raised designation, and no womb/kinship network supplies a relational peer.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001305/B001","root_001332/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_c9c0fd9e34234b612b4a","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"112:4:2\",\"root_001332\",[\"B002\"]],[\"112:4:4\",\"root_001305\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Hiçbir varlık, Allah'ın konumuna denk bir yer veya rütbe işgal edemez.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001305/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_d47b955c655c7285b416","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"112:4:4\",\"root_001305\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Denkliğin reddi bütün ilişkilerin reddi değildir; merhamet ve dayanak arama ilişkileri sürer, ancak hiçbir varlık Allah'la eşit karşılık veya karşılıklı konum paylaşamaz.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001305/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_e9d238b89de3f90a377b","text":"2. **Equivalence is denied as matching response, not only as static similarity.** Lexical evidence: `ك ف ء B001` includes equality, suitability, recompense by like-for-like response, and facing/alternation; `ص م د B001` supplies one-way resort; `ر ح م B001` supplies beneficence. Structural cue: the surah has already mapped movement toward `الصمد`; the final clause refuses a `كفو` \"for Him.\" Assigned roles: `الصمد` is the asymmetrical destination; `كفو` would be the entity able to answer, match, face, or reciprocate on equal terms; `أحد` under negation removes every candidate. Abductive move: I infer that if resort and mercy might imply reciprocal relation, `كفو` tests whether anything can meet God as equal respondent. Reading change: the ayah denies not only a look-alike but any like-for-like counterpart capable of equal opposition, recompense, or alternation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000017/B002","root_001305/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_eeafae8c49714444130c","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"112:4:4\",\"root_001305\",[\"B001\"]],[\"112:4:5\",\"root_000017\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Kapanış, açılıştaki ilahi adın bir adaş veya eş taşıyıcıya açılamayacağını da geriye dönük olarak kesinleştirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_efff5c75026f881b319d","text":"No later ayah exists in the clipped packet, so no later retrospective activation or correction emerged for this fixed ayah.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
